CREATE ROLE "ROLE_CRIARUSUARIOS";

  GRANT DROP USER TO "ROLE_CRIARUSUARIOS";
  GRANT ALTER USER TO "ROLE_CRIARUSUARIOS";
  GRANT CREATE USER TO "ROLE_CRIARUSUARIOS";
